Launches the binary as a child process with a controlled environment:\n       PORT                       -> free loopback port chosen at runtime\n       ADMINUSER                  -> freshly generated \"user:password\" pair\n       SIGNALK_NODE_CONFIG_DIR    -> fresh temp directory we own\n       HOME                       -> fresh temp directory (keeps ~/.signalk\n                                     from being used as a fallback)\n  2. Launches the binary as a child process with a controlled environment:\n       PORT                       -> free loopback port chosen at runtime\n       ADMINUSER                  -> freshly generated \"user:password\" pair\n       SIGNALK_NODE_CONFIG_DIR    -> fresh temp directory we own\n       HOME                       -> fresh temp directory (keeps ~/.signalk\n                                     from being used as a fallback)\n  2. Scans the SIGNALK_NODE_CONFIG_DIR the server used for files containing\n     the marker, to determine whether the supplied content was accepted into\n     the server's configuration storage.\n  6. In this build it is registered **without** the admin-auth middleware that guards `/restore`, `/backup`, etc. — the script sends it fully unauthenticated (no `Authorization` header, no cookie, no prior session) and never retries with credentials if rejected.\n- **Apply** — `POST /skServer/restore` (admin-auth required; 401 without a valid session) copies whitelisted archive members (`settings.json`, `defaults.json`, `security.json`, `package.json`, `baseDeltas.json`) selected by a JSON body into `app.config.configPath` (= `SIGNALK_NODE_CONFIG_DIR`).\n\nThe script:\n\n1.
